INTEREST RATE RISK                                                        利率風險

The Group’s pledged bank deposits, finance lease receivables              本集團的已抵押銀行存款、融資租賃應收款項及
and convertible notes payable bore fixed interest rates. The              應付可換股票據以固定利率計息。本集團的銀行

Exposure to interest rate risk existed on those outstanding               浮動利率計息的未償還結餘將面對利率風險。本
balances subject to floating interest rate when there are                 集團的政策為在協定框架內管理其利率風險，以
unexpected adverse interest rate movements. The Group’s                   確保在出現重大利率變動時不會承受過高風險，
policy was to manage its interest rate risk within an agreed              並於有需要時大致固定利率。
framework to ensure that there were no undue exposures to

fixed when necessary.

PROSPECTS                                                               前景

In 2020, while continuing to take most of the advantage as              二零二零年，本集團將繼續發揮香港全球金融中
Hong Kong being a global financial center and the strong                心的優勢以及公司管理團隊在國內政府、企業及
support to the Company’s management team from government,               金融機構的強信用背書，準確把握「新基建」的東
enterprises and financial institutions in Mainland China, the           風，深化政企合作，設立產業引導基金、擔保基
Group would also ride the tide of “neo infrastructure”, intensify       金。本集團亦會參與粵港澳大灣區、國內其他地
government-enterprise cooperation, and establish industry-              區 5G 網絡、數據中心等數字化基礎設施建設，把
guide funds and guarantee funds. Apart from those, the Group            握當中機遇，為當地傳統行業轉型升級賦能，助
would expect to participate in the opportunities created by the         力當地數字經濟發展。
development of digitised infrastructure such as 5G networks
and data centers in Guangdong-Hong Kong-Macao Greater
Bay Area and other regions in Mainland China with a vision to
propel the transformation, upgrade local traditional industries
and support the development of the local digital economy.


The Group will pursue the “investment bank + finance” two-              本集團將實行「投行 + 金融」雙輪驅動模式，依托
pronged approach and pinpoint business opportunities                    集團金融服務牌照優勢及管理團隊豐富行業經驗，
emerged in the wake of the pandemic by leveraging on the                精准洞察疫情過後涌現的商機，幫助更多國內企
strength created from licenses for financial services the Group         業開展海外併購業務，搭建多元化的融資體系，
holds and its management team’s extensive industry                      拓展境外融資渠道，優化債務結構，降低融資成
experience. In addition, the Group will assist more domestic            本。
enterprises in mergers and acquisitions business overseas, while
diversifying its financing system, expanding financing channels
abroad, optimising the debt structure and reducing financing
costs.


Meanwhile, as the Group fully supports the “Belt and Road”              同時，集團將積極充分響應「一帶一路」發展倡議，
initiative, the Group will acquire traditional financial institutions   通過聚合股東強大的產業、平台資源，以股權投
with full licenses in those countries along the “Belt and Road”         資、運營權接管等形式，深度布局「一帶一路」沿

operation by consolidating strong industrial support from               步涉足互聯網金融這一快速崛起的新興經濟體。
Shareholders and platform resources, and leveraging on which,
the Group could gradually extend its footprint to internet

Summary of the Board Diversity Policy                                     董事會多元化政策摘要

of its strategic objectives and its sustainable development. In           從多方面考慮董事會成員多元化，包括但不限於

not limited to gender, age, cultural and educational                      原則，並在考慮人選時以客觀條件充分顧及董事
background, professional experience, skills and knowledge. All            會成員多元化之裨益。

be considered against objective criteria, having due regard for

Measurable Objectives                                                     可計量目標

•    the Company’s actual and expected financial performance;          •     本公司之實際及估計財務表現；

     Company;

     equity and the relevant financial covenants;                            相關財務契諾；

     imposed by any contract entered into by the Company or                  派付股息施加之任何限制；
     its subsidiaries;

     and future expansion plans;                                             劃；

     Company and other internal or external factors that may                 可能對本公司業務或財務表現及狀況構成影
     have an impact on the business or financial performance                 響之內部或外部因素；及
     and position of the Company; and


•    any other factors that the Board may deem appropriate.            •     董事會可能視為適當之任何其他因素。

     KONG FINANCIAL REPORTING STANDARDS                                         （「香港財務報告準則」）
                                                                                           （續）
     (“HKFRSs”) (continued)

     2.1 Application of new/revised HKFRSs (continued)                          2.1 應用新訂╱經修訂香港財務報告準
                                                                                  則（續）
           Impact of the adoption of HKFRS 16                                     採納香港財務報告準則第 16 號之影響


           HKFRS 16, “Leases” replaces the previous standards                     香港財務報告準則第 16 號「租賃」取代
           HKAS 17 “Leases” and effective for the current year                    過 往 準 則 香 港 會 計 準 則 第 17 號「 租
           beginning on or after 1 April 2019. The Group has                      賃」，並於二零一九年四月一日或以後
           applied HKFRS 16 for the first time in the current                     開始之本年度生效。本集團已於本年
           year.                                                                  度首次應用香港財務報告準則第16號。


           Leases are recognised as a right-of-use asset and a                    租賃於租賃資產可供本集團使用當日
           corresponding liability at the date at which the                       確認為使用權資產及相關負債。各租
           leased asset is available for use by the Group. Each                   賃付款獲分配至負債及融資成本。融
           lease payment is allocated between the liability and                   資成本在租賃期間於損益入賬，以得
           finance costs. The finance costs are charged to profit                 出各期間負債餘下結餘之定期固定利
           or loss over the lease period so as to produce a                       率。使用權資產於資產可使用年期與
           constant periodic rate of interest on the remaining                    租期兩者間之較短者內按直線法折舊。
           balance of the liability for each period. The right-of-
           use asset is depreciated over the shorter of the
           asset’s useful life and the lease term on a straight-
           line basis.


           Assets and liabilities arising from a lease are initially              租賃產生之資產及負債初步以現值基
           measured on a present value basis. Lease liabilities                   準計量。租賃負債包括下列租賃付款
           include the net present value of the following lease                   之現值淨額：
           payments:


           •       fixed payments (including in-substance fixed                   •   固定付款（包括實質固定付款）減
                   payments), less any lease incentives receivable;                   任何應收租賃獎勵；


           •       variable lease payments that are based on an                   •   基於指數或利率之可變租賃付
                   index or a rate;                                                   款；


           •       amounts expected to be payable by the lessee                   •   承租人根據剩餘價值擔保預期應
                   under residual value guarantees;                                   付之款項；

               the results if HKAS 17 had been applied during
               the year.


               In the consolidated statement of cash flows, the                         於 綜 合 現 金 流 量 表 內， 本 集 團
               Group as a lessee is required to split rentals                           （作為承租人）須將資本化租賃項
               paid under capitalised leases into their capital                         下已付租金分拆為資本部分及利
               element and interest element. These elements                             息部分。與先前根據香港會計準
               are classified as financing cash outflows, similar                       則第 17 號分類為融資租賃之租賃
               to how leases previously classified as finance                           相關處理方式類似，有關部分獲
               leases under HKAS 17 were treated, rather than                           分類為融資現金流出而非經營現
               as operating cash outflows, as was the case for                          金流出，而香港會計準則第 17 號
               operating leases under HKAS 17. The adoption                             項下經營租賃亦然。因此，採納
               of HKFRS 16 therefore results in a change in                             香港財務報告準則第 16 號導致綜
               presentation of cash flows within the                                    合現金流量表內現金流量之呈列
               consolidated statement of cash flows.                                    方式出現變動。

     (“HKFRSs”) (continued)

                                                                                  則（續）


           HK(IFRIC)-Int 23 — Uncertainty over Income Tax                         香港（國際財務報告詮釋委員會）— 詮
           Treatments                                                             釋第 23 號 — 所得稅處理之不確定性


           The Interpretation supports the requirements of                        該詮釋透過提供有關如何反映所得稅
           HKAS 12, Income Taxes, by providing guidance over                      處理之不確定性之指引，為香港會計
           how to reflect the effects of uncertainty in accounting                準則第12號
                                                                                       「所得稅」
                                                                                           之要求作出補充。
           for income taxes.


           Under the Interpretation, the entity shall determine                   根據該詮釋，實體須以可對不確定性
           whether to consider each uncertain tax treatment                       結果作出較佳預測之方式，決定單獨
           separately or together based on which approach                         或一併考慮各個不確定之稅務處理。
           better predicts the resolution of the uncertainty. The                 實體亦須假設稅務機關將審查其有權
           entity shall also assume the tax authority will examine                審查之金額，且進行審查時充分掌握
           amounts that it has a right to examine and have full                   所有相關資料。倘實體認為稅務機關
           knowledge of all related information when making                       可能會接受不確定之稅務處理，則實
           those examinations. If the entity determines it is                     體須按照其稅務登記文件計量即期及
           probable that the tax authority will accept an                         遞延稅項。倘實體認為上述情況不大
           uncertain tax treatment, then the entity should                        可能發生，則釐定稅項時之不確定性
           measure current and deferred tax in line with its tax                  須透過「最有可能的金額」或「預期價值」
           filings. If the entity determines it is not probable,                  法反映，以可對不確定性結果作出較
           then the uncertainty in the determination of tax is                    佳預測之方式為準。
           reflected using either the “most likely amount” or
           the “expected value” approach, whichever better
           predicts the resolution of the uncertainty.


           The adoption of these amendments has no impact                         由於本集團於所得稅會計處理方面並
           on these financial statements as the Group has no                      無 不確定性，故採納有關修訂對該等
           uncertainty in accounting for income taxes.                            財務報表未有造成影響。

3.   SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING POLICIES                                      3.    主要會計政策
     The consolidated financial statements have been prepared                   綜合財務報表乃根據香港會計師公會（「香港
     in accordance with Hong Kong Financial Reporting                           會計師公會」）頒佈之香港財務報告準則（「香
     Standards (“HKFRSs”) (which include all Hong Kong                          港財務報告準則」，包括所有香港財務報告
     Financial Reporting Standards, Hong Kong Accounting                        準則、香港會計準則（「香港會計準則」）及詮
     Standards (“HKASs”) and Interpretations) issued by the                     釋）、香港公認會計原則及香港公司條例之
     Hong Kong Institute of Certified Public Accountants                        披露規定而編製。該等綜合財務報表亦符合
     (“HKICPA”), accounting principles generally accepted in                    香港聯合交易所有限公司證券上市規則之適
     Hong Kong and the disclosure requirements of the Hong                      用披露條文。綜合財務報表按歷史成本法編
     Kong Companies Ordinance. These consolidated financial                     製，惟按公平值計量之股本投資除外。誠如
     statements also comply with the applicable disclosure                      附註 3 進一步闡釋，分類為持作出售之資產
     provisions of the Rules Governing the Listing of Securities                按其賬面值與公平值減出售成本兩者中之較

           the joint arrangement.                                                   債。

     provide evidence of impairment of the assets transferred
     they are recognised immediately to profit or loss.


     Any premium paid for an investment in a joint venture                   就合營企業所支付高於本集團分佔所收購可
     above the fair value of the Group’s share of the identifiable           識別資產、負債及或然負債之任何溢價會撥
     assets, liabilities and contingent liabilities acquired is              充資本，並計入於合營企業之投資之賬面
     capitalised and included in the carrying amount of the                  值。倘有客觀證據顯示於合營企業之投資出
     investment in joint venture. Where there is objective                   現減值，則投資之賬面值會按測試其他非金
     evidence that the investment in a joint venture has been                融資產相同之方式進行減值測試。
     impaired, the carrying amount of the investment is tested
     for impairment in the same way as other non-financial
     assets.


     The Group’s interests in joint ventures are stated at cost              本集團於合營企業之權益按成本減去減值虧
     less impairment losses, if any. Results of joint ventures are           損（如有）列賬。本集團按已收及應收股息為
     accounted for by the Group on the basis of dividends                    基準就合營企業之業績入賬。
     received and receivable.

     business (see the accounting policy above) less                            有）列賬。
     accumulated impairment losses, if any.


     For the purposes of impairment testing, goodwill is                        就減值測試而言，商譽獲分配至預期受惠於
     allocated to each of the Group’s cash-generating units (or                 合併協同效益之本集團各現金產生單位（或
     groups of cash-generating units) that is expected to                       現金產生單位組別），而該單位或單位組別
     benefit from the synergies of the combination, which                       指就內部管理目的監控商譽之最低水平且不
     represent the lowest level at which the goodwill is                        超過經營分部。
     monitored for internal management purposes and not
     larger than operating segment.


     A cash-generating unit (or group of cash-generating units)                 獲分配商譽之現金產生單位（或現金產生單
     to which goodwill has been allocated is tested for                         位組別）每年或倘有跡象顯示有關單位可能
     impairment annually or more frequently there is indication                 出現減值時更頻繁地進行減值測試。就報告
     that the unit may be impaired. For goodwill arising on an                  期間進行收購所產生之商譽而言，獲分配商
     acquisition in a reporting period, the cash-generating unit                譽之現金產生單位（或現金產生單位組別）於
     (or group of cash-generating units) to which goodwill has                  該報告期末之前進行減值測試。倘相關可收
     been allocated is tested for impairment before the end of                  回金額低於賬面值，則減值虧損首先分配以
     that reporting period. If the recoverable amount is less                   減少任何商譽之賬面值，繼而以該單位（或
     than its carrying amount, the impairment loss is allocated                 現金產生單位組別）內各資產之賬面值為基
     first to reduce the carrying amount of any goodwill and                    準按比例分配至其他資產。
     then to the other assets on a pro-rata basis based on the
     carrying amount of each asset in the unit (or group of
     cash-generating units).

     Revenue recognition                                                   收入確認


     Revenue from contracts with customers is recognised                   客戶合約之收入於貨品或服務控制權轉移至
     when control of goods or services is transferred to the               客戶時確認，其金額反映本集團預期就換取
     customers at an amount that reflects the consideration to             該等貨品或服務有權收取之代價，惟不包括
     which the Group expects to be entitled in exchange for                代表第三方收取之金額。收入不包括增值稅
     those goods or services, excluding those amounts                      或其他銷售稅，並已扣除任何貿易折扣。
     collected on behalf of third parties. Revenue excludes
     value added tax or other sales taxes and is after deduction
     of any trade discounts.


     Depending on the terms of the contract and the laws that              根據合約條款及適用於合約之法例，貨品或
     apply to the contract, control of the goods or service may            服務控制權可隨時間或於某一時間點轉移。
     be transferred over time or at a point in time. Control of            倘本集團履約時出現以下情況，則貨品或服
     the goods or service is transferred over time if the Group’s          務控制權隨時間轉移：
     performance:


     •    provides all of the benefits received and consumed               •     客戶同時取得並耗用所有利益；
          simultaneously by the customer;


     •    creates or enhances an asset that the customer                   •     產生或提升一項資產，而該項資產於
          controls as the Group performs; or                                     本集團履約時由客戶控制；或


     •    does not create an asset with an alternative use to              •     並未產生讓本集團有替代用途之資產，
          the Group and the Group has an enforceable right to                    且本集團對迄今已完成履約之付款具
          payment for performance completed to date.                             有可強制執行之權利。


     If control of the goods or services transfers over time,              倘貨品或服務控制權隨時間轉移，則於合約
     revenue is recognised over the period of the contract by              期間參照全面履行該履約責任之進度確認收
     reference to the progress towards complete satisfaction of            入。否則，收入於客戶獲得貨品或服務控制
     that performance obligation. Otherwise, revenue is                    權之時間點確認。
     recognised at a point in time when the customer obtains
     control of the goods or service.

     Foreign currencies                                                      外幣


     In preparing the financial statements of each individual                於編製各集團個別實體的財務報表時，以該
     group entity, transactions in currencies other than the                 實體功能貨幣以外的貨幣（外幣）進行的交
     functional currency of that entity (foreign currencies) are             易，乃按其交易日期當日的匯率確認。於報
     recognised at the rates of exchanges prevailing on the                  告期末，以外幣為單位的貨幣項目按該日的
     dates of the transactions. At the end of the reporting                  現行匯率重新換算。以外幣為單位及以公平
     period, monetary items denominated in foreign currencies                值計量的非貨幣項目按釐定公平值當日的現
     are retranslated at the rates prevailing at that date. Non-             行匯率重新換算。以外幣為單位及以歷史成
     monetary items carried at fair value that are denominated               本計量的非貨幣項目，則毋須重新換算。
     in foreign currencies are retranslated at the rates prevailing
     on the date when the fair value was determined. Non-
     monetary items that are measured in terms of historical
     cost in a foreign currency are not retranslated.


     Exchange differences arising on the settlement of                       因結算及重新換算貨幣項目所產生的匯兌差
     monetary items and on the retranslation of monetary                     額，於其產生的期間內於損益確認。
     items, are recognised in profit or loss in the period in
     which they arise.

     Taxation                                                                稅項


     Income tax expense represents the sum of the tax                        所得稅開支指目前應繳的稅項及遞延稅項的
     currently payable and deferred tax.                                     總額。


     The tax currently payable is based on taxable profit for the            目前應繳稅項乃按年內應課稅溢利計算。由
     year. Taxable profit differs from ‘profit (loss) before                 於應課稅溢利不包括其他年度的應課稅或可
     taxation’ as reported in the consolidated statement of                  扣稅的收支項目，亦不包括毋須課稅及不可
     profit or loss and other comprehensive income because of                扣稅項目，故應課稅溢利有別於綜合損益及
     income or expense that are taxable or deductible in other               其他全面收益表內所呈報的「除稅前溢利（虧
     years and items that are never taxable and deductible. The              損）」。本集團目前的稅項負債乃採用於報告
     Group’s liability for current tax is calculated using tax rates         期末已頒佈或實際上已頒佈的稅率計算。
     that have been enacted or substantively enacted by the
     end of the reporting period.


     Deferred tax is recognised on temporary differences                     遞延稅項按綜合財務報表內資產及負債的賬
     between the carrying amounts of assets and liabilities in               面值與計算應課稅溢利所採用的相應稅基之
     the consolidated financial statements and the                           間的暫時差額而確認。一般會就所有應課稅
     corresponding tax base used in the computation of                       暫時差額確認遞延稅項負債。遞延稅項資產
     taxable profit. Deferred tax liabilities are generally                  一般會以該等可動用之可扣減暫時差額將可
     recognised for all taxable temporary differences. Deferred              能抵銷應課稅溢利為限，就所有可扣減暫時
     tax assets are generally recognised for all deductible                  差額而確認。倘暫時差額乃因在一項不影響
     temporary difference to the extent that it is probable that             應課稅溢利或會計溢利的交易中初步確認的
     taxable profits will be available against which those                   資產及負債（業務合併除外）而產生，則不會
     deductible temporary differences can be utilised. Such                  確認有關遞延稅項資產及負債。
     deferred tax assets and liabilities are not recognised if the
     temporary difference arises from the initial recognition
     (other than in a business combination) of assets and
     liabilities in a transaction that affects neither the taxable
     profit nor the accounting profit.

     Deferred tax liabilities are recognised for taxable                        遞延稅項負債乃就於附屬公司投資的相關應
     temporary differences associated with investments in                       課稅暫時差額而確認，惟倘本集團能夠控制
     subsidiaries, except where the Group is able to control the                暫時差額撥回及暫時差額有可能不會於可見
     reversal of the temporary difference and it is probable that               將來撥回則除外。與該等投資及權益相關的
     the temporary difference will not reverse in the                           可扣稅暫時差額所產生的遞延稅項資產，僅
     foreseeable future. Deferred tax assets arising from                       於可能有足夠應課稅溢利可以應用暫時差額
     deductible temporary differences associated with such                      的利益且預計於可見將來可以撥回時方會確
     investments and interests are only recognised to the                       認。

     temporary differences and they are expected to reverse in
     the foreseeable future.


     The carrying amount of deferred tax assets is reviewed at                  遞延稅項資產的賬面值會於報告期末檢討及
     the end of the reporting period and reduced to the extent                  進行扣減，直至不再可能有足夠應課稅溢利
     that it is no longer probable that sufficient taxable profits              可供收回全部或部分資產為止。
     will be available to allow all or part of the asset to be
     recovered.


     Deferred tax assets and liabilities are measured at the tax                遞延稅項資產及負債根據於報告期末已頒佈
     rates that are expected to apply in the period in which the                或實際上已頒佈之稅率（及稅法），按清償負
     liability is settled or the asset is realised, based on tax rate           債或變現資產期間的預期適用稅率計量。
     (and tax laws) that have been enacted or substantively
     enacted by the end of the reporting period.


     The measurement of deferred tax liabilities and assets                     遞延稅項負債及資產的計量反映按照本集團
     reflects the tax consequences that would follow from the                   預期於報告期末收回或清償其資產及負債賬
     manner in which the Group expects, at the end of the                       面值的方式而得出的稅務後果。
     reporting period, to recover or settle the carrying amount
     of its assets and liabilities.

     except when they relate to items that are recognised in                    其他全面收入確認或直接於權益確認的項目
     other comprehensive income or directly in equity, in which                 有關則除外，在此情況下，即期及 遞延稅項
     case, the current and deferred tax are also recognised in                  亦分別於其他全面收入確認或直接於權益確
     other comprehensive income or directly in equity                           認。
     respectively.

     which includes both leasehold land and building elements,                  部份）付款，本集團會根據評估各部分擁有
     the Group assesses the classification of each element as a                 權之絕大部分風險及回報是否已轉移至本集
     finance or an operating lease separately based on the                      團而將其劃分為融資或經營租賃，除非肯定
     assessment as to whether substantially all the risks and                   兩個部分均屬經營租賃，於此情況下，則整
     rewards incidental to ownership of each element have                       項物業將入賬為經營租賃。具體而言，整筆
     been transferred to the Group, unless it is clear that both                代價（包括任何一筆過預付款項）於初始確認
     elements are operating leases in which case the entire                     時按租賃土地部分及樓宇部分中之租賃權益
     property is accounted as an operating lease. Specifically,                 相對公平值比例於租賃土地及樓宇部分之間
     the entire consideration (including any lump-sum upfront                   分配。
     payments) are allocated between the leasehold land and
     the building elements in proportion to the relative fair
     values of the leasehold interests in the land element and
     building element of the lease at initial recognition.


     When the lease payments cannot be allocated reliably                       當租賃款項不能在租賃土地及樓宇部分之間
     between the leasehold land and building elements, the                      作可靠分配，則整個物業一般會按猶如租賃
     entire property is generally classified as if the leasehold                土地為融資租賃項下之方式作出分類。
     land is under finance lease.


     Non-current assets and disposal groups held for sale                       持作出售之非流動資產及出售組別


     Non-current assets and disposal groups are classified as                   倘非流動資產及出售組別將主要透過出售交
     held for sale if their carrying amounts will be recovered                  易而非透過持續使用而收回賬面值，則分類
     principally through a sale transaction rather than through                 為持作出售。僅於資產或出售組別可供以現
     continuing use. Such classification requires the asset or the              狀即時出售（只受出售有關資產或出售組別
     disposal group must be available for immediate sale in its                 之慣常條款約束）且極有可能成交時方被視
     present condition subject only to terms that are usual and                 為符合此項條件。管理層必須致力完成出
     customary for sales of such asset or disposal group and its                售，預期自分類日期起計一年內符合資格確
     sale is highly probable. Management must be committed                      認為一項已完成銷售交易。持作出售之非流
     to the sale, which should be expected to qualify for                       動資產按其先前賬面值與公平值減出售成本

     date of classification. Non-current assets as held for sale

           designated upon initial recognition at fair value                          資產包括持作買賣之金融資產、於初
           through profit or loss, or financial assets mandatorily                    始確認時指定為以公平值計量且其變
           required to be measured at fair value. Financial                           化計入損益之金融資產或強制要求按
           assets are classified as held for trading if they are                      公平值計量之金融資產。倘收購金融
           acquired for the purpose of selling or repurchasing in                     資產之目的為作短期出售或購回用途，
           the near term. Derivatives, including separated                            則該等金融資產分類為持作買賣。除
           embedded derivatives, are also classified as held for                      非衍生工具（包括獨立嵌入式衍生工具）
           trading unless they are designated as effective                            被指定為實際對沖工具，否則亦分類
           hedging instruments. Financial assets with cash flows                      為持作買賣。現金流量並非僅為支付
           that are not solely payments of principal and interest                     本金及利息之金融資產分類為以公平
           are classified and measured at fair value through                          值計量且其變化計入損益，而不論其
           profit or loss, irrespective of the business model.                        業務模式如何。儘管存在上述將債務
           Notwithstanding the criteria for debt instruments to                       工具分類為按攤銷成本或以公平值計

           so eliminates, or significantly reduces, an accounting

     (ii)   Impairment loss on financial assets                                 (ii)   金融資產之減值虧損


            The Group recognises loss allowances for expected                          本集團就貿易應收款項、應收貸款、
            credit loss (“ECL”) on trade receivables, loan                             其他應收款項及融資租賃應收款項確
            receivables, other receivables and finance lease                           認預期信貸虧損（「預期信貸虧損」）之
            receivables. The ECLs are measured on either of the                        虧損撥備。預期信貸虧損採用以下基
            following bases: (i) 12 months ECLs: these are the                         準 計 量：(i)12 個 月 預 期 信 貸 虧 損：即
            ECLs that result from possible default events within                       於報告日期後 12 個月內可能發生之違
            the 12 months after the reporting date; and (ii)                           約事件將導致之預期信貸虧損；及 (ii)
            lifetime ECLs: these are ECLs that result from all                         全期預期信貸虧損：即金融工具預期
            possible default events over the expected life of a                        使用期內所有可能違約事件將導致之
            financial instrument. The maximum period                                   預期信貸虧損。估計預期信貸虧損時
            considered when estimating ECLs is the maximum                             所考慮最長期間為本集團面對信貸風
            contractual period over which the Group is exposed                         險之最長合約期間。
            to credit risk.

            ECLs are a probability-weighted estimate of credit                         預期信貸虧損為信貸虧損之概率加權
            losses. Credit losses are measured as the difference                       估計。信貸虧損乃按本集團根據合約
            between all contractual cash flows that are due to the                     應付所有合約現金流量與本集團預期
            Group in accordance with the contract and all the                          收取所有現金流量之間的差額計量。
            cash flows that the Group expects to receive. The                          該差額其後按與資產原有實際利率相
            shortfall is then discounted at an approximation to                        若之水平貼現。
            the assets’ original effective interest rate.

            The Group has elected to measure loss allowances                           本集團已選擇使用香港財務報告準則
            for trade receivables from client arising from                             第 9 號之簡化方法計量建築機械業務、
            construction machinery business, cash clients and                          現金客戶及其他金融服務所產生貿易
            other financial services using HKFRS 9 simplified                          應收款 項之虧損撥備，並根據全期預
            approach and has calculated ECLs based on lifetime                         期信貸虧損計算預期信貸虧損。本集
            ECLs. The Group has established a provision matrix                         團已設立根據本集團過往信貸虧損經
            that is based on the Group’s historical credit loss                        驗計算之撥備矩陣，並按債務人特定
            experience, adjusted for forward-looking factors                           前瞻性因素及經濟環境作出調整。
            specific to the debtors and the economic
            environment.

            For trade receivables from margin clients and other                        來自保證金客戶之貿易應收款項及其
            debt financial assets, the ECLs are based on the                           他債務金融資產之預期信貸虧損以 12
            12-months ECLs. However, when there has been a                             個月預期信貸虧損為基準。然而，倘
            significant increase in credit risk since origination, the                 產生以來信貸風險顯著增加，則撥備
            allowance will be based on the lifetime ECLs.                              將以全期預期信貸虧損為基準。

     In general, when loan receivables or its related instalments               量。一般而言，倘應收貸款或其相關分期付

32. CONVERTIBLE NOTES                                               32. 可換股票據

   As set out in Note 44, the Company issued convertible                  誠如附註 44 所載，於二零二零年一月三十一
   notes with aggregate principal amount of HK$213,000,000                日，本 公 司 發 行 本 金 總 額 為 213,000,000 港
   as the purchase consideration for the acquisition of equity            元之可換股票據，作為收購昊天信貸股權之
   interests in Hao Tian Credit on 31 January 2020.                       購買代價。


   The convertible notes have a maturity date of 3 years from             可換股票據之到期日為發行日期起計 3 年（即
   the date of issue (i.e. 30 January 2023), bear interest rate           二 零 二 三 年 一 月 三 十 日），按 年 利 率 5% 計
   of 5% per annum, and are payable at each anniversary year              息，並須於每個發行週年日支付（「可換股票
   of issue in arrear (the “Convertible Notes”). The                      據」）。可換股票據附帶權利可按換股價每股
   Convertible Notes contain a right to convert at a maximum              0.35 港 元 兌 換 最 多 608,571,428 股 本 公 司 股
   of 608,571,428 shares of the Company at the conversion                 份。除非先前已轉換、購買或贖回，否則本
   price of HK$0.35 per share. Unless previously converted or             公司將於到期日按相等於可換股票據當時未
   purchased or redeemed, the Company would redeem the                    償還本金額 100% 之贖回金額贖回可換股票
   Convertible Notes on the maturity date at the redemption               據。
   amount which was 100% of the principal amount of the
   Convertible Notes then outstanding.


   The Company may at any time before maturity and from                   本公司可於到期日前隨時及不時按可換股票
   time to time redeem the Convertible Notes (in whole but                據本金額連同累計至提前贖回日期之未償還
   not in part) at the principal amount of such Convertible               利息贖回全部（而非部分）可換股票據。
   Notes together with unpaid interest accrued up to the
   date of such early redemption.


   Since the exercise of conversion option embedded in the                由於行使可換股票據內含換股權將透過以定
   Convertible Notes would result in settlement by the                    額現金換取本公司定額股份而償付，故內含
   exchange of a fixed amount of cash for a fixed number of               換股權入賬列為權益工具。發行可換股票據
   shares of the Company, the embedded conversion option                  之本金總額 213,000,000 港元已分為負債及權
   was therefore accounted for as an equity instrument. The               益部分。根據獨立專業合資格估值師行羅馬
   aggregate principal amount of HK$213,000,000 from the                  國際評估有限公司之估值，於發行可換股票
   issue of the Convertible Notes had been split into liability           據時可換股票據之公平值、負債部分及剩餘
   and equity components. On the issue of the Convertible                 價值     分別釐定為約 209,322,000
                                                                           （即權益部分）
   Notes, the fair value of the Convertible Notes, the liability          港元、145,186,000港元及64,136,000港元。
   component and the residual value being equity                          負債部分按攤銷成本入賬列為金融負債，直
   component were determined as approximately                             至終止或轉換為止。計入權益之換股權之賬
   HK$209,322,000, HK$145,186,000 and HK$64,136,000                       面值不會於往後期間重新計量。
   respectively, based on the valuation by Roma Appraisals
   Limited, an independent firm of professionally qualified

39. CAPITAL RISK MANAGEMENT                                               39. 資本風險管理

     The Group manages its capital to ensure that entities in                   本集團管理其資本，以確保本集團之實體可
     the Group will be able to continue as a going concern                      持續經營，並透過優化債務與權益之間的均
     while maximising the return to shareholders through the                    衡狀態為股東帶來最大回報。本集團之整體
     optimisation of the debt and equity balance. The Group’s                   策略自上年度起維持不變。
     overall strategy remains unchanged from prior year.


     The Group is not subject to any externally imposed capital                 本集團並無被施加任何外在資本規定，惟若
     requirements except for certain subsidiaries engaged in                    干從事證券及期貨買賣及經紀服務並為受香
     securities and futures dealings and broking, which are                     港證券及期貨事務監察委員會（「證監會」）規
     regulated by the Hong Kong Securities and Futures                          管之附屬公司除外。
     Commission (the “SFC”).


     During the year, the subsidiaries of the Group which are                   於本年度，本集團之附屬公司受各監管機構
     subject to minimum capital requirements imposed by                         施加之最低資本要求所規限，並符合所有最
     respective regulatory authorities, complied with all the                   低資本要求。
     minimum capital requirements.


     The capital structure of the Group consists of debts, which                本集團的資本結構包括債務，而債務包括借
     include the borrowings, loan from a director, obligation                   貸、來自一名董事之貸款、融資租賃責任及
     under finance leases and equity attributable to owners of                  本公司擁有人應佔權益（包括股本及儲備）。
     the Company, comprising share capital and reserves.


     The management reviews the capital structure by                            管理層通過考慮資金成本及各類資本之相關
     considering the cost of capital and the risks associated                   風險而檢討資本架構。因此，本集團將透過
     with each class of capital. In view of this, the Group will                發行新股份及發行新債務或贖回現有債務達
     balance its overall capital structure through new share                    致整體資本架構之平衡。本集團之整體策略
     issues as well as the issue of new debt or the redemption                  全年維持不變。
     of existing debt. The Group’s overall strategy remains
     unchanged throughout the year.

   Liquidity risk                                                         流動資金風險


   In the management of the liquidity risk, the Group                     本集團通過監管並將現金及現金等價物維持
   monitors and maintains a level of cash and cash                        於管理層認為足以應付本集團營運之水平管
   equivalents deemed adequate by the management to                       理流動資金風險，以及減低現金流量波動之
   finance the operations of the Group and mitigate the                   影響。管理層監管動用借貸的情況並確保遵
   effects of fluctuations in cash flows. The management                  守貸款契諾。
   monitors the utilisation of borrowings and ensures
   compliance with loan covenants.


   In addition, certain subsidiaries of the Company is                    此外，本公司若干附屬公司受證監會規管，
   regulated by SFC and is subject to certain requirements                並須遵守證券及期貨（財政資源）規則項下若
   under the Securities and Futures (Financial Resources)                 干規定，因此，本集團須監察此附屬公司之
